Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a cfl algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 674. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 804/4.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cfl space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 674 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 804. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any cfl module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 845. The projective resolution has length at most 201 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on cfl structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 845. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 201. QED.
